New error\nsrc/specify_cli/core/errors.py:12:families subclass it and override ``error_code``; families predating this\nsrc/specify_cli/core/errors.py:20:__all__ = [\"StructuredError\"]\nsrc/specify_cli/core/errors.py:23:class StructuredError(RuntimeError):\nsrc/specify_cli/core/errors.py:24:    \"\"\"Base for exceptions carrying a stable, machine-readable ``error_code``.\nsrc/specify_cli/core/errors.py:26:    Subclasses override ``error_code`` with a stable identifier so consumers\nsrc/specify_cli/core/errors.py:33:    error_code: str = \"\"\nsrc/specify_cli/core/errors.py:35:    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\nsrc/specify_cli/core/errors.py:37:        return {\"error_code\": self.error_code, \"message\": str(self)}\nsrc/specify_cli/core/errors.py:40:class PlacementResolutionRequired(StructuredError):\nsrc/specify_cli/core/errors.py:56:    shared this exact ``error_code``).\nsrc/specify_cli/core/errors.py:59:    error_code = \"PLACEMENT_RESOLUTION_REQUIRED\"\n\"\"\"Shared base for structured exceptions carrying a stable ``error_code``.\n\nSeveral exception families across the codebase expose a machine-readable\n``error_code`` so consumers branch on the typed value rather than\nsubstring-matching human-readable messages (NFR-007). Before this base\nthose families re-decided the idiom each time (class-attr constant,\n``__init__`` parameter, or full contextual ``to_dict()``), producing the\ndivergence flagged in #1893.\n\n:class:`StructuredError` is the minimal shared contract: a default\n``error_code`` class attribute and a default :meth:`to_dict`. New error\nfamilies subclass it and override ``error_code``; families predating this\nbase adopt it on next touch (no big-bang migration).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any\n\n__all__ = [\"StructuredError\"]\n\n\nclass StructuredError(RuntimeError):\n    \"\"\"Base for exceptions carrying a stable, machine-readable ``error_code``.\n\n    Subclasses override ``error_code`` with a stable identifier so consumers\n    branch on the typed value rather than substring-matching the message\n    (NFR-007, #1893). :meth:`to_dict` returns a JSON-serializable envelope;\n    subclasses that carry extra contextual fields override it and extend the\n    base payload.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    error_code: str = \"\"\n\n    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Return a JSON-serializable representation for tooling.\"\"\"\n        return {\"error_code\": self.error_code, \"message\": str(self)}\n\n\nclass PlacementResolutionRequired(StructuredError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a write site cannot resolve its canonical placement (D11).\n\n    Fail-closed replacement for the forbidden checkout-derived commit grammars\n    (``_get_current_branch() or planning_branch`` / the silent \"conservative\n    legacy preflight\" degradation) — coord-primary-partition-lock WP03 / D11,\n    contracts/seam-api.md. A genuine placement-resolution failure (missing\n    mission, corrupt state, a ``coordination_branch`` declared in meta.json but\n    torn down in git, ...) must never silently commit to whatever branch the\n    operator happens to have checked out. A genuinely-legacy, coordination-less\n    mission resolves successfully; this error fires only on a REAL failure,\n    which must be fixed via migration/backfill (e.g. ``spec-kitty doctor\n    workspaces --fix``), never papered over with a silent runtime fallback.\n\n    Single canonical home (consolidated from the two identical per-command\n    copies formerly in implement.py + agent/mission_record_analysis.py, which\n    shared this exact ``error_code``).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    error_code = \"PLACEMENT_RESOLUTION_REQUIRED\"\n\n    An explicit ``--topology`` always wins. Otherwise: PR-bound missions and\n    missions created on the repository's primary branch keep the historical\n    ``coord`` default (a coordination branch is minted). A mission created on\n    a non-primary feature/fork branch without ``--pr-bound`` defaults to\n    ``single_branch`` instead — minting a coordination branch there just to\n    have the operator manually flatten it is the exact friction #2581 closes.\n    \"\"\"\n    if explicit_topology is not None:\n        return explicit_topology\n    if pr_bound:\n        return MissionTopology.COORD\n    if repo_root is None or current_branch is None:\n        return MissionTopology.COORD\n\n    from specify_cli.core.git_ops import resolve_primary_branch\n\n    primary_branch = resolve_primary_branch(repo_root)\n    if current_branch == primary_branch:\n        return MissionTopology.COORD\n    return MissionTopology.SINGLE_BRANCH\n\n\ndef _print_worktree_navigation_hint(mission_slug: str, error_msg: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Print the 'run from main repo' hint when a worktree error blocked creation.\"\"\"\n    if \"worktree\" not in error_msg.lower():\n        return\n    # Route through the ``mission`` module so the ``mission.locate_project_root``\n    # patch seam keeps reaching this branch after relocation.\n    from specify_cli.cli.commands.agent import mission as _mission\n\n    cwd = Path.cwd().resolve()\n    main_repo = _mission.locate_project_root(cwd)\n    if main_repo is None:\n        # Fallback: try .worktrees path heuristic.\n        for i, part in enumerate(cwd.parts):\n            if part == \".worktrees\":\n                main_repo = Path(*cwd.parts[:i])\n                break\n    if main_repo is not None:\n        console.print(\"\\n[cyan]Run from the main repository instead:[/cyan]\")\n        console.print(f\"  cd {main_repo}\")\n        console.print(f\"  spec-kitty agent mission create {mission_slug}\")\n\n\ndef _run_create_core_phase(\n    *,\n    repo_root: Path | None,\n    mission_slug: str,\n    resolved_mission_type: str | None,\n    target_branch: str | None,\n    friendly_name: str | None,\n    purpose_tldr: str | None,\n    purpose_context: str | None,\n    force_recreate_coordination_branch: bool,\n    json_output: bool,\n    topology: MissionTopology = MissionTopology.COORD,\n) -> MissionCreationResult:\n    \"\"\"Invoke ``create_mission_core`` with the deterministic error funnel.\n\n    Exits 1 (with the appropriate structured payload) on the three documented\n    failure classes: coordination-branch divergence (NFR-007 stable error_code),\n    a ``MissionCreationError`` (with worktree navigation hint), or any other\n    unexpected exception.\n    \"\"\"\n    from specify_cli.core.mission_creation import (\n        MissionCreationError,\n        create_mission_core,\n    )\n    from specify_cli.missions._create import CoordinationBranchDiverged\n\n        # for WP06 (see contracts/mission_create_clean_output.contract.md).\n        mark_invocation_succeeded()\n    else:\n        console.print(f\"[green]✓[/green] Mission created: {result.mission_slug}\")\n        console.print(f\"   Title: {result.meta.get('friendly_name', '')}\")\n        console.print(f\"   TLDR: {result.meta.get('purpose_tldr', '')}\")\n        console.print(f\"   Context: {result.meta.get('purpose_context', '')}\")\n        console.print(f\"   Directory: {result.feature_dir}\")\n        # Issue #846: spec.md is no longer auto-committed at create time.\n        # The agent commits it from /spec-kitty.specify after writing substantive content.\n        console.print(f\"   Meta committed to {result.target_branch}; spec.md scaffold left untracked\")\n        console.print(\"   [yellow]Scaffold only:[/yellow] run [cyan]/spec-kitty.specify <intent>[/cyan] in your agent, or edit and commit spec.md before planning.\")\n\n\ndef create_mission(\n    mission_slug: Annotated[str, typer.Argument(help=\"Mission slug (e.g., 'user-auth')\")],\n    mission_type: Annotated[\n        str | None,\n        typer.Option(\"--mission-type\", help=\"Mission type (e.g., 'documentation', 'software-dev')\"),\n    ] = None,\n    mission: Annotated[\n        str | None,\n        typer.Option(\"--mission\", hidden=True, help=\"(deprecated) Use --mission-type\"),\n    ] = None,\n    json_output: Annotated[bool, typer.Option(\"--json\", help=\"Output JSON format\")] = False,\n    target_branch: Annotated[str | None, typer.Option(\"--target-branch\", help=\"Target branch (defaults to current branch)\")] = None,\n    friendly_name: Annotated[str | None, typer.Option(\"--friendly-name\", help=\"Human-friendly mission title\")] = None,\n    purpose_tldr: Annotated[str | None, typer.Option(\"--purpose-tldr\", help=\"One-line stakeholder TLDR for the mission\")] = None,\n    purpose_context: Annotated[str | None, typer.Option(\"--purpose-context\", help=\"Short stakeholder-facing paragraph for the mission\")] = None,\n    pr_bound: Annotated[bool, typer.Option(\"--pr-bound/--no-pr-bound\", help=\"Mark mission as PR-bound (gate fires on merge_target_branch)\")] = False,\n    topology: Annotated[\n        MissionTopology | None,\n        typer.Option(\n            \"--topology\",\n            help=(\n                \"Create-time mission shape: single_branch | lanes | coord | \"\n                \"lanes_with_coord. Coordination-bearing shapes (coord, \"\n                \"lanes_with_coord) mint a coordination branch; branch-flat \"\n                \"shapes (single_branch, lanes) do not. Default: context-derived \"\n                \"(#2581) — coord on the primary branch, with --pr-bound, or \"\n                \"when explicitly requested; single_branch on a non-primary \"\n                \"feature/fork branch without --pr-bound.\"\n            ),\n        ),\n    ] = None,\n    branch_strategy: Annotated[\n        str | None,\n        typer.Option(\n            \"--branch-strategy\",\n            help=\"Branch-strategy gate control (e.g., 'already-confirmed' to bypass the prompt)\",\n        ),\n    ] = None,\n    start_branch: Annotated[\n        str | None,\n        typer.Option(\n            \"--start-branch\",\n            help=\"Create or switch to this branch before mission files are written\",\n        ),\n    ] = None,\n    force_recreate_coordination_branch: Annotated[\n        bool,\n        typer.Option(\n            \"--force-recreate-coordination-branch\",\n            help=(\n                \"Delete and recreate the per-mission coordination branch if it \"\n                \"already exists and has diverged from the target. Operator \"\n                \"escape hatch; never used by automation.\"\n            ),\n        ),\n    ] = False,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create new mission directory structure in the project root checkout.\n\n    This command is designed for AI agents to call programmatically.\n    Creates mission directory in kitty-specs/ and commits to the current branch.\n\n    Examples:\n        spec-kitty agent mission create \"new-dashboard\" --json\n    \"\"\"\n    # Deferred import keeps this leaf free of an import cycle while honoring the\n    # ``mission.locate_project_root`` / ``mission.get_current_branch`` /\n    # ``mission._switch_to_start_branch`` patch targets the tests rely on.\n    from specify_cli.cli.commands.agent import mission as _mission\n\n    repo_root = _mission.locate_project_root()\n\n    _resolve_start_branch_phase(\n        repo_root=repo_root,\n        start_branch=start_branch,\n        target_branch=target_branch,\n        json_output=json_output,\n    )\n\n    resolved_mission_type = _resolve_mission_type_phase(\n        mission_type=mission_type,\n        mission=mission,\n        json_output=json_output,\n    )\n\n    current_branch = _mission.get_current_branch(repo_root)\n    _enforce_branch_strategy_gate_phase(\n        pr_bound=pr_bound,\n        current_branch=current_branch,\n        target_branch=target_branch,\n        branch_strategy=branch_strategy,\n        start_branch=start_branch,\n        json_output=json_output,\n    )\n\n    resolved_topology = _resolve_default_topology_phase(\n        explicit_topology=topology,\n        repo_root=repo_root,\n        current_branch=current_branch,\n        pr_bound=pr_bound,\n    )\n\n    # Import the tracker package here (NOT at module scope) so ``tracker/__init__.py``\n    # registers ``consume_pending_origin_impl`` with ``core.adapters`` BEFORE\n    # ``create_mission_core`` runs ``consume_pending_origin`` (register-before-use,\n    # T012). Keeping this import inside the command body — rather than at module\n    # scope — keeps the whole tracker/sync/SaaS stack off the CLI cold-start path\n    # (NFR-003), while preserving the CLI-layer placement so no CORE→INTEGRATION\n    # import edge is introduced in ``core/mission_creation.py`` (#614 leak fix).\n    import specify_cli.tracker  # noqa: F401  (import side-effect: origin-consumer registration)\n\n    result = _run_create_core_phase(\n        repo_root=repo_root,\n        mission_slug=mission_slug,\n        resolved_mission_type=resolved_mission_type,\n        target_branch=target_branch,\n        friendly_name=friendly_name,\n        purpose_tldr=purpose_tldr,\n        purpose_context=purpose_context,\n        topology=resolved_topology,\n        force_recreate_coordination_branch=force_recreate_coordination_branch,\n        json_output=json_output,\n    )\n\n    _persist_pr_bound_phase(result, pr_bound=pr_bound)\n    _emit_create_result_phase(\n        result,\n        resolved_mission_type=resolved_mission_type,\n        json_output=json_output,\n    )\n\ndef decide_next(agent: str, mission_slug: str, result: str, repo_root):\n    \"\"\"Patchable lazy wrapper for the next mutation engine.\"\"\"\n    from runtime.next.decision import decide_next as _decide_next\n\n    return _decide_next(agent, mission_slug, result, repo_root)\n\n\ndef _runtime_bridge_module():\n    \"\"\"Return the patched bridge when tests/consumers installed one.\"\"\"\n    return sys.modules.get(\"runtime.next.runtime_bridge\") or importlib.import_module(\n        \"runtime.next.runtime_bridge\"\n    )\n\n\n@require_main_repo\ndef next_step(\n    agent: Annotated[str | None, typer.Option(\"--agent\", help=\"Agent name (required for advancing mode)\")] = None,\n    result: Annotated[\n        str | None,\n        typer.Option(\n            \"--result\",\n            help=(\"Result of previous step: success|failed|blocked. If omitted, returns current state without advancing (query mode).\"),\n        ),\n    ] = None,\n    mission: Annotated[str | None, typer.Option(\"--mission\", help=\"Mission slug\")] = None,\n    json_output: Annotated[bool, typer.Option(\"--json\", help=\"Output JSON decision only\")] = False,\n    answer: Annotated[str | None, typer.Option(\"--answer\", help=\"Answer to a pending decision\")] = None,\n    decision_id: Annotated[str | None, typer.Option(\"--decision-id\", help=\"Decision ID (required if multiple pending)\")] = None,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Decide and emit the next agent action for the current mission.\n\n    Agents call this command repeatedly in a loop.  The system inspects the\n    mission state machine, evaluates guards, and returns a deterministic\n    decision with an action and prompt file.\n\n    Examples:\n        spec-kitty next --mission 034-my-feature --json                            # query mode\n        spec-kitty next --agent claude --mission 034-my-feature --result success --json\n        spec-kitty next --agent codex --mission 034-my-feature\n        spec-kitty next --agent gemini --mission 034-my-feature --result failed --json\n        spec-kitty next --agent claude --mission 034-my-feature --answer \"yes\" --result success --json\n        spec-kitty next --agent claude --mission 034-my-feature --answer \"approve\" --decision-id \"input:review\" --result success --json\n    \"\"\"\n    _maybe_emit_runtime_notice(json_output)\n\n    repo_root = locate_project_root()\n    if repo_root is None:\n        print(\"Error: Could not locate project root\", file=sys.stderr)\n        raise typer.Exit(1)\n\n    # FR-006 caller contract: charter preflight runs BEFORE any state\n    # mutation. On failure, print blocked_reason and exit 1 — the runtime\n    # decision engine is never entered. Query mode (result is None) is\n    # read-only and follows the dashboard's \"log + warn + continue\" path\n    # so that operators can inspect mission state in repos whose charter\n    # has not yet been synthesized (e.g., fresh clones, test envs).\n    from pathlib import Path as _Path\n\n    _run_charter_preflight_for_next(_Path(str(repo_root)), advancing=result is not None, json_output=json_output)\n\n    from runtime.next.runtime_bridge import MissionNotFoundError as _MissionNotFoundError\n    from specify_cli.missions._read_path_resolver import (\n        StatusReadPathNotFound as _StatusReadPathNotFound,\n    )\n\n    try:\n        mission_slug = _resolve_mission_slug(mission, repo_root)\n    except _StatusReadPathNotFound as _exc:\n        # FR-001 / C-IC02: preserve the typed read-path error (code + checked\n        # paths + read-path remediation) instead of collapsing to MISSION_NOT_FOUND.\n        _emit_read_path_error(_exc, json_output)\n        raise typer.Exit(1) from _exc\n    except _MissionNotFoundError as _exc:\n        _emit_mission_not_found_error(_exc.handle, json_output)\n        raise typer.Exit(1) from _exc\n    seam_target = placement_seam(repo_root, mission_slug).write_target(MissionArtifactKind.SPEC)\n    safe_commit(\n        repo_root=repo_root,\n        worktree_root=repo_root,\n        target=seam_target,\n        message=commit_msg,\n        paths=(file_path,),\n        capability=GuardCapability.STANDARD,\n    )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Public API\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef create_mission_core(\n    repo_root: Path | None,\n    mission_slug: str,\n    *,\n    mission: str | None = None,\n    target_branch: str | None = None,\n    friendly_name: str | None = None,\n    purpose_tldr: str | None = None,\n    purpose_context: str | None = None,\n    topology: MissionTopology = MissionTopology.COORD,\n    force_recreate_coordination_branch: bool = False,\n    allow_worktree_context: bool = False,\n) -> MissionCreationResult:\n    \"\"\"Create a new feature with all scaffolding.\n\n    This is the programmatic API for feature creation.  Unlike the CLI\n    command, it returns a structured result and raises domain exceptions\n    instead of calling ``typer.Exit()``.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    repo_root:\n        Absolute path to the project root (must contain ``.kittify/`` and\n        ``kitty-specs/``).  When *None*, ``locate_project_root()`` is called\n        automatically.\n    mission_slug:\n        Bare slug such as ``\"user-auth\"`` or ``\"068-feature\"`` (kebab-case).\n    mission:\n        Optional mission key (e.g. ``\"documentation\"``, ``\"software-dev\"``).\n        Defaults to ``\"software-dev\"`` when not provided.\n    target_branch:\n        Explicit target branch for the feature.  When *None* the current\n        git branch is used.\n    friendly_name:\n        Optional mission title shown on operator-facing surfaces. When omitted,\n        it is derived from ``mission_slug``.\n    purpose_tldr:\n        Optional one-line product/CXO summary for the mission. When omitted,\n        it defaults to the resolved ``friendly_name``.\n    purpose_context:\n        Optional short paragraph explaining the mission in stakeholder terms.\n        When omitted, it defaults to a branch-aware summary sentence.\n    topology:\n        Operator's create-time mission shape (#2218). Defaults to\n        :attr:`MissionTopology.COORD` for backward-compat. The coordination\n        branch is minted only for the coordination-bearing shapes\n        (``COORD`` / ``LANES_WITH_COORD``); ``SINGLE_BRANCH`` / ``LANES`` skip\n        the mint and never write ``coordination_branch``. The explicit choice\n        is persisted verbatim into ``meta.json`` — never re-derived from\n        ``classify_topology`` (which cannot reproduce ``LANES`` pre-finalize).\n    allow_worktree_context:\n        Bypass the worktree-context guard (step 2) that otherwise raises when\n        the *process* ``cwd`` resolves inside a git worktree. Defaults to\n        ``False``, preserving the interactive/CLI guard that protects\n        operators from accidentally scaffolding a mission inside a lane\n        worktree instead of the project root checkout. Intended for\n        programmatic callers — notably ``tests/_factories.make_mission()``\n        (FR-008) — that pass an explicit ``repo_root`` pointing at an\n        isolated (often temporary) repository while the test process itself\n        happens to be running from within a lane worktree checkout, which is\n        this project's normal execution context for its own test suite.\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    MissionCreationResult\n        Structured result with all paths and metadata.\n\n    Raises\n    ------\n    MissionCreationError\n        On any validation or creation failure.\n    charter.activation.pack_context.CharterPackConfigError\n        When the project has no activated mission types (an absent or empty\n        ``mission_type_activations`` set). This is the WP04 fail-closed at the\n        mission-create / mission-type-use boundary: ``PackContext``\n        construction is total, so the \"provision your charter\" error is\n        raised here rather than at construction time.\n    specify_cli.runtime.resolver.TemplateConfigurationError\n        If the activated mission type cannot resolve its configured ``spec``\n        template. Resolution happens before any mission state is created.\n    \"\"\"\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 1. Input validation\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    if not KEBAB_CASE_PATTERN.match(mission_slug):\n        raise MissionCreationError(\n            f\"Invalid feature slug '{mission_slug}'. \"\n            \"Must be kebab-case (lowercase letters, numbers, hyphens only).\"\n            \"\\n\\nValid examples:\"\n            \"\\n  - user-auth\"\n            \"\\n  - fix-bug-123\"\n            \"\\n  - 068-feature-name\"\n            \"\\n  - new-dashboard\"\n            \"\\n\\nInvalid examples:\"\n            \"\\n  - User-Auth (uppercase)\"\n            \"\\n  - user_auth (underscores)\"\n        )\n\n    friendly_name_was_provided = friendly_name is not None\n    normalized_friendly_name = \" \".join((friendly_name or \"\").split())\n    if friendly_name_was_provided and not normalized_friendly_name:\n        raise MissionCreationError(\"Mission creation requires a non-empty friendly_name.\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 2. Context guards\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    cwd = Path.cwd().resolve()\n    if not allow_worktree_context and is_worktree_context(cwd):\n        raise MissionCreationError(\"Cannot create missions from inside a worktree. Run from the project root checkout.\")\n\n    resolved_root = repo_root\n    if resolved_root is None:\n        resolved_root = locate_project_root()\n    if resolved_root is None:\n        raise MissionCreationError(\"Could not locate project root. Run from within spec-kitty repository.\")\n\n    if not is_git_repo(resolved_root):\n        raise MissionCreationError(\"Not in a git repository. Mission creation requires git.\")\n\n    current_branch = get_current_branch(resolved_root)\n    if not current_branch or current_branch == \"HEAD\":\n        raise MissionCreationError(\"Must be on a branch to create missions (detached HEAD detected).\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 3. Resolve planning branch\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

resolve_ownership_claim(\n    claimed_checkout: Path | None, *, resolved_primary: Path\n) -> OwnershipClaim:\n    \"\"\"Validate an explicit checkout-ownership request against git topology.\n\n    No git subprocess runs when ``claimed_checkout`` is ``None``. Opted-in\n    validation fails closed into a structured result whenever git topology\n    cannot be read.\n    \"\"\"\n    primary = resolved_primary.resolve()\n    if claimed_checkout is None:\n exc:\n        return _claim(\n            claimed,\n            primary,\n            OwnershipValidationResult.BROKEN_POINTER,\n            opted_in=True,\n            detail=(\n dataclasses import FrozenInstanceError\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom unittest.mock import patch\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom specify_cli.core import checkout_ownership\nfrom repositories, mirroring\n# tests/git_ops/test_safe_commit_helper_integration.py's `git_repo` fixture.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _run_git(args: list[str], cwd: Path) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:\n    return subprocess.run(\n        [\"git\", *args],\n        cwd=cwd,\n        capture_output=True,\n        text=True,\n        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n        errors=\"replace\",\n        check=True,\n    )\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef git_repo(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Create a real, initialized git repository with one commit.\"\"\"\n    repo = tmp_path / \"primary\"\n    repo.mkdir()\n    _run_git([\"init\"], cwd=repo)\n    _run_git([\"config\", \"user.email\", \"test@example.com\"], cwd=repo)\n    _run_git([\"config\", \"user.name\", \"Test User\"], cwd=repo)\n    (repo / \"README.md\").write_text(\"# Test Repo\\n\")\n    _run_git([\"add\", \"README.md\"], cwd=repo)\n    _run_git([\"commit\", \"-m\", \"Initial commit\"], cwd=repo)\n    _run_git([\"branch\", \"-M\", \"main\"], cwd=repo)\n    return repo\n\n\ndef _add_worktree(repo: Path, path: Path, branch: str) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Create a real linked worktree of ``repo`` at ``path`` on a new branch.\"\"\"\n    _run_git([\"worktree\", \"add\", \"-b\", branch, str(path)], cwd=repo)\n    return path\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# T002 — UNOWNED_NO_OPT_IN (no claim, zero subprocess calls, NFR-001)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_no_claim_returns_unowned_no_opt_in(\n    git_repo: Path,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"``claimed_checkout=None`` returns UNOWNED_NO_OPT_IN with no git calls.\"\"\"\n    with patch(\"subprocess.run\") as run:\n        claim = resolve_ownership_claim(None, resolved_primary=git_repo)\n\n    assert claim.validation_result is OwnershipValidationResult.UNOWNED_NO_OPT_IN\n    assert claim.opted_in is False\n    assert claim.detail\n    assert str(git_repo.resolve()) in claim.detail\n    run.assert_not_called()\n\n\ndef test_no_claim_error_for_claim_maps_to_unowned_error(git_repo: Path) -> None:\n    claim = resolve_ownership_claim(None, resolved_primary=git_repo)\n    err = error_for_claim(claim)\n    assert isinstance(err, UnownedNoOptInError)\n    assert isinstance(err, CheckoutOwnershipError)\n    assert err.validation_result is OwnershipValidationResult.UNOWNED_NO_OPT_IN\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# T002 — trivial self-ownership (primary claiming itself) is OWNED\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_primary_self_ownership_is_owned(git_repo: Path) -> None:\n    claim = resolve_ownership_claim(git_repo, resolved_primary=git_repo)\n\n    assert claim.validation_result is OwnershipValidationResult.OWNED\n    assert claim.opted_in is True\n    assert claim.claimed_checkout == git_repo.resolve()\n    assert claim.resolved_primary == git_repo.resolve()\n    assert error_for_claim(claim) is None\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# T002/T003 — valid linked (non-nested) worktree is OWNED\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_valid_linked_worktree_is_owned(git_repo: Path, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    worktree = _add_worktree(git_repo, tmp_path / \"linked-wt\", \"feature-a\")\n\n    claim = resolve_ownership_claim(worktree, resolved_primary=git_repo)\n\n    assert claim.validation_result is OwnershipValidationResult.OWNED\n    assert claim.opted_in is True\n    assert claim.claimed_checkout == worktree.resolve()\n    assert error_for_claim(claim) is None\n\n\ndef test_managed_linked_worktree_under_primary_is_owned(git_repo: Path) -> None:\n    worktrees_dir = git_repo / \".worktrees\"\n    worktrees_dir.mkdir()\n    worktree = _add_worktree(git_repo, worktrees_dir / \"linked-wt\", \"feature-managed\")\n\n    claim = resolve_ownership_claim(worktree, resolved_primary=git_repo)\n\n    assert claim.validation_result is OwnershipValidationResult.OWNED\n\n\ndef test_public_worktree_comparator_reuses_safe_commit_topology(\n    git_repo: Path, tmp_path: Path\n) -> None:\n    worktree = _add_worktree(git_repo, tmp_path / worktree (registry-based, not `.worktrees`-literal) is NESTED\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_nested_worktree_is_nested(git_repo: Path, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    outer = _add_worktree(git_repo, tmp_path / \"outer-wt\", \"feature-outer\")\n    inner = _add_worktree(git_repo, outer / \"nested-inner-wt\", \"feature-inner\")\n\n    claim = resolve_ownership_claim(inner, resolved_primary=git_repo)\n\n    assert claim.validation_result is OwnershipValidationResult.NESTED\n    assert claim.opted_in is True\n    assert claim.detail\n    assert str(inner.resolve()) in claim.detail\n    assert str(outer.resolve()) in claim.detail\n\n    err = error_for_claim(claim)\n    assert isinstance(err, NestedCheckoutError)\n    assert err.validation_result is OwnershipValidationResult.NESTED\n\n\ndef test_nested_worktree_uses_generic_registry_not_dot_worktrees_literal(\n    git_repo: Path, tmp_path: Path\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"C-006: nested detection must work for paths with NO ``.worktrees`` segment.\n\n    ``outer``/``inner`` below live under a plain ``tmp_path`` tree — neither\n    path contains a literal ``.worktrees`` component, so a detector keyed on\n    that literal would miss this case entirely (the exact C-006 gap this WP\n    closes).\n    \"\"\"\n    outer = _add_worktree(git_repo, tmp_path / \"generic-outer\", \"feature-generic-outer\")\n    inner = _add_worktree(git_repo, outer / \"generic-inner\", \"feature-generic-inner\")\n    assert \".worktrees\" not in inner.parts\n\n    claim = resolve_ownership_claim(inner, resolved_primary=git_repo)\n\n    assert claim.validation_result is OwnershipValidationResult.NESTED\n\n\n# \"Test User\"], cwd=foreign)\n    (foreign / \"README.md\").write_text(\"# Foreign Repo\\n\")\n    _run_git([\"add\", \"README.md\"], cwd=foreign)\n    _run_git([\"commit\", \"-m\", \"Initial commit\"], cwd=foreign)\n\n    claim = resolve_ownership_claim(foreign, resolved_primary=git_repo)\n\n    assert claim.validation_result is OwnershipValidationResult.FOREIGN_OR_MISMATCHED\n    assert \".git\").resolve()) in claim.detail\n\n    err = error_for_claim(claim)\n    assert isinstance(err, ForeignOrMismatchedCheckoutError)\n    assert err.validation_result is does not exist.\n    git_file = worktree / \".git\"\n    assert git_file.is_file()\n    git_file.write_text(\"gitdir: /nonexistent/path/.git/worktrees/to-corrupt-wt\\n\")\n\n    claim =
